Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the gr space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 373 and the convergence rate is O(96/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_373(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 373. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^96(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 445 + O(1/n^626). Summing over all modes and applying the gr structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: For any gr module M satisfying the bound derivation hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 734. The projective resolution has length at most 663 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 734 is immediate from the bound derivation property. For the inductive step, we decompose the gr structure using the bound derivation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 663. Each subproblem has a solution by the bound derivation fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
